---
id: ''
name: 入驻式+UL CL UPF+辅锚点UPF配置实例(含和MPF对接) (SDN)
ne_type: UEG
type: feature
description: UEG部署在园区，作为企业专用UL CL UPF+辅锚点UPF。企业园区终端签约专有DNN（APN）。  * 对于app流量过HUAWEI MPF的第三方APP，UPF到MPF采用VXLAN隧道，然后由MPF与第三方APP互通；或UPF到企业园区采用VPN专线，流量不过MPF。 * 对于园区服务器（app流量不过HUAWEI MPF），UPF到企业园区采用VPN专线。  UEG使用标卡与DCGW连接。DCGW之间使用E-Trunk+EVPN或M-LAG组网，UEG使用Eth-trunk接口，一个Eth-trunk接口的成员端口，连接到同一台DCGW上。  UEG通过BGP over静态路由+BFD单臂echo与对端网元（gNodeB/SMF/企业服务器/MPF）实现网络互通。
tags: []
risk_level: ''
doc_source: ["网络部署","园区入驻+地市共享（连接+计算）","5GC toB 部署边缘站点（站点为“风筝S”，带计算）","UEG初始配置（风筝S）","初始配置与调测（入驻式UPF）","初始配置与调测（入驻式边缘UPF，SDN）","配置实例","入驻式+UL CL UPF+辅锚点UPF配置实例(含和MPF对接) (SDN)"]
refs: []
metadata:
  DepOnLiveCfg: true
allowed-tools:
- getParameterValue('LST AUTOCONFIG')
---
<!-- @mml:block= annotations -->
```text
## 操作场景
UEG部署在园区，作为企业专用UL CL UPF+辅锚点UPF。企业园区终端签约专有DNN（APN）。* 对于app流量过HUAWEI MPF的第三方APP，UPF到MPF采用VXLAN隧道，然后由MPF与第三方APP互通；或UPF到企业园区采用VPN专线，流量不过MPF。* 对于园区服务器（app流量不过HUAWEI MPF），UPF到企业园区采用VPN专线。UEG使用标卡与DCGW连接。DCGW之间使用E-Trunk+EVPN或M-LAG组网，UEG使用Eth-trunk接口，一个Eth-trunk接口的成员端口，连接到同一台DCGW上。UEG通过BGP over静态路由+BFD单臂echo与对端网元（gNodeB/SMF/企业服务器/MPF）实现网络互通。

## 必备事项
前提条件

* 具备管理员权限的操作人员已经登录华为操作维护系统（登录MAE客户端或登录OM Portal）。
* VNF与网络实体之间的网络环境已经构建完成。
```

## 3 命令模板定义
<!-- @mml:block= commands -->

### 1 配置基础数据。

### 配置基础数据包括：修改网元浮动IP、修改公共参数等。详细配置与组网无关，具体请参见《参考：详细配置指导 > 配置基础数据》章节。

### 2 激活全局BFD。

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_bfd_list -%}
    SET BFD:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

### 3 激活全局BGP。

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_bgp_list -%}
    SET BGP:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    BGPENABLE={{item.BGPENABLE}},
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BGPENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BGPENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.ASNUM is not none -%}
            ASNUM="{{item.ASNUM}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

### 4 DHCP分配IPv6地址时 （如：SDN场景），必须设置“DUID生成方式（DUIDTYPE）”为“MAC\_PLUS\_VLAN”。

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_dhcp6clientduid_list -%}
    SET DHCP6CLIENTDUID:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    DUIDTYPE={{item.DUIDTYPE}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

### 5 关闭自动配置开关。

#### 5.1 查询自动配置开关。

#### 如果“自动配置开关”值为“TRUE”，需要执行5.b和5.c关闭自动配置开关。

#### 如果“自动配置开关”值为“FALSE”，说明已关闭自动配置开关，请跳转到6。

#### 5.2 查询自动部署维护助手“autoscaling\_autoconfig.py”的状态，确保状态为“准备好状态（Ready）”或者查询无该脚本，以保证当前设备不在自动部署过程中。因为若系统当前有正在进行的自动部署，而自动部署生效有时延，此时若关闭自动配置开关，会影响系统配置效果。

#### 5.3 关闭自动配置开关。自动化配置命令只有在自动配置开关为关闭的状态下才可执行，因此需要提前关闭。

```jinja2
{%- set LST_AUTOCONFIG=getParameterValue('LST AUTOCONFIG') -%}
{%- if LST_AUTOCONFIG=='TRUE' -%}
    {%- for item in set_autoconfig_list -%}
        SET AUTOCONFIG:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        SWITCHFLAG={{item.SWITCHFLAG}},
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

#### 6.1 配置VPN实例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_l3vpninst_list -%}

    VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2

    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFRD is not none -%}
        VRFRD="{{item.VRFRD}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_interface_list -%}

    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2

    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",

    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2

    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    IFIPADDR="{{item.IFIPADDR}}",
    SUBNETMASK="{{item.SUBNETMASK}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRTYPE is not none -%}
        ADDRTYPE={{item.ADDRTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_ifipv6enable_list -%}
```

#### 6.3 配置BGP VPN实例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgpvrf_list -%}
    ADD BGPVRF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.DEFAULTAFTYPE is not none -%}
        DEFAULTAFTYPE={{item.DEFAULTAFTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ROUTERID is not none -%}
        ROUTERID="{{item.ROUTERID}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 6.4 增加BGP VPN实例地址族。UEG对接2个负荷分担的BGP PEER时，“MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE”设置为“2”。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgpvrfaf_list -%}
    ADD BGPVRFAF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=ipv4uni/ipv6uni 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE in ['ipv4uni', 'ipv6uni'] -%}
        {%- if item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE is not none -%}
            MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE={{item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 6.5 配置BGP对等体。此处以对接2个IPv4v6双栈的BGP对等体为例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgppeer_list -%}
    ADD BGPPEER:
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE is not none -%}
        ADDRESSTYPE={{item.ADDRESSTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4'] -%}
        PEERADDR="{{item.PEERADDR}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=ipv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE == 'ipv6' -%}
        PEERADDRV6="{{item.PEERADDRV6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.REMOTEAS is not none -%}
            REMOTEAS="{{item.REMOTEAS}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFADDR is not none -%}
            LOCALIFADDR="{{item.LOCALIFADDR}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFNAME is not none -%}
            LOCALIFNAME="{{item.LOCALIFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.EBGPMAXHOP is not none -%}
            EBGPMAXHOP={{item.EBGPMAXHOP}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 6.6 配置BGP引入wlr路由。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_importroute_list -%}
    ADD IMPORTROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    IMPORTPROTOCOL={{item.IMPORTPROTOCOL}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}

    {%- endif -%}

        MEDENABLE={{item.MEDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 6.7 添加以太网隧道多虚拟网卡自动化配置模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingethtrunk_list -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGETHTRUNK:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    ETHTRUNKTMPID={{item.ETHTRUNKTMPID}},
    VNICLIST="{{item.VNICLIST}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 6.8 配置外联口自动部署模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingservice_list -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSERVICE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VPNNAME is not none -%}
        VPNNAME="{{item.VPNNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE is not none -%}
        AUTOCFGIFTYPE={{item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.VLANID is not none -%}
        VLANID={{item.VLANID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：AUTOCFGIFTYPE=ETHTRUNK 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE == 'ETHTRUNK' -%}
        ETHTRUNKTMPID={{item.ETHTRUNKTMPID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE4={{item.IPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv6' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE6={{item.IPALLOCTYPE6}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPALLOCTYPE6=DHCP 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 == 'DHCP' -%}
        {%- if item.ADDRMODE is not none -%}
            ADDRMODE={{item.ADDRMODE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 如果规划外联口使用IPv4v6双栈地址，则分别执行该命令对同一vNIC配置IPv4和IPv6地址。

#### 外联口配置IPv6地址时，“ADDRMODE”必须设置为“NETWOKSEGMENT\_MODE”。

#### 6.9 增加BFD会话自动部署模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingbfd_list -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGBFD:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    TEMPLATENAME="{{item.TEMPLATENAME}}",
    BFDTYPE={{item.BFDTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.DETECTMULTI is not none -%}
        DETECTMULTI={{item.DETECTMULTI}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：BFDTYPE=Static 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：BFDTYPE=Static 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        DESTADDR4="{{item.DESTADDR4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        DESTADDR6="{{item.DESTADDR6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDTYPE=Static 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        {%- if item.MINECHORXINT is not none -%}
            MINECHORXINT={{item.MINECHORXINT}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDTYPE=Static 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        {%- if item.ONEARMECHO is not none -%}
            ONEARMECHO={{item.ONEARMECHO}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 6.10 增加静态路由自动部署模板。

##### 6.10.1 //“PREFIX4”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
        BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
        DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
            BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 6.10.2 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX4”设置为“0.0.0.0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_2 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
        MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
            VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.PREFERENCE is not none -%}
            PREFERENCE={{item.PREFERENCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
            BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
            DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
                NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
            {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
                BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

##### 6.10.3 //“PREFIX6”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_3 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
        BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
        DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        PREFIX6="{{item.PREFIX6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
            NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
            BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 6.10.4 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX6”设置为“0:0:0:0:0:0:0:0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

#### 7.3 配置BGP VPN实例。


#### 7.5 配置BGP对等体。此处以对接2个IPv4v6双栈的BGP对等体为例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgppeer_list_4 -%}

    {%- endif -%}

    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE == 'ipv6' -%}
        PEERADDRV6="{{item.PEERADDRV6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.REMOTEAS is not none -%}
            REMOTEAS="{{item.REMOTEAS}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFADDR is not none -%}
            LOCALIFADDR="{{item.LOCALIFADDR}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFNAME is not none -%}
            LOCALIFNAME="{{item.LOCALIFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}

        {%- if item.EBGPMAXHOP is not none -%}

    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 7.10 增加静态路由自动部署模板。

##### 7.10.1 //“PREFIX4”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_6 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
        BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
        DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
            BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 7.10.2 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX4”设置为“0.0.0.0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_8 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
        MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
            VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.PREFERENCE is not none -%}
            PREFERENCE={{item.PREFERENCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
            BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
            DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
                NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
            {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
                BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

#### 8.7 添加以太网隧道多虚拟网卡自动化配置模板。与N3共用物理口时，请跳过本步骤。

```jinja2
{%- if is_optional_ethernet_tunnel_vnic_template -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingethtrunk_list_2 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGETHTRUNK:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        ETHTRUNKTMPID={{item.ETHTRUNKTMPID}},
        VNICLIST="{{item.VNICLIST}}",
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

#### 8.8 配置外联口自动部署模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingservice_list_4 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSERVICE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VPNNAME is not none -%}
        VPNNAME="{{item.VPNNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE is not none -%}
        AUTOCFGIFTYPE={{item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.VLANID is not none -%}
        VLANID={{item.VLANID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：AUTOCFGIFTYPE=ETHTRUNK 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE == 'ETHTRUNK' -%}
        ETHTRUNKTMPID={{item.ETHTRUNKTMPID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE4={{item.IPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv6' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE6={{item.IPALLOCTYPE6}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPALLOCTYPE6=DHCP 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 == 'DHCP' -%}
        {%- if item.ADDRMODE is not none -%}
            ADDRMODE={{item.ADDRMODE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 8.9 增加BFD会话自动部署模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingbfd_list_4 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGBFD:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    TEMPLATENAME="{{item.TEMPLATENAME}}",
    BFDTYPE={{item.BFDTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.DETECTMULTI is not none -%}
        DETECTMULTI={{item.DETECTMULTI}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：BFDTYPE=Static 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：BFDTYPE=Static 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        DESTADDR4="{{item.DESTADDR4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        DESTADDR6="{{item.DESTADDR6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDTYPE=Static 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        {%- if item.MINECHORXINT is not none -%}
            MINECHORXINT={{item.MINECHORXINT}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDTYPE=Static 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        {%- if item.ONEARMECHO is not none -%}
            ONEARMECHO={{item.ONEARMECHO}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 8.10 增加静态路由自动部署模板。

##### 8.10.1 //“PREFIX4”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_12 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
        BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
        DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
            BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 8.10.2 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX4”设置为“0.0.0.0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_14 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
        MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
            VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.PREFERENCE is not none -%}
            PREFERENCE={{item.PREFERENCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
            BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
            DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
                NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
            {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
                BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

##### 8.10.3 //“PREFIX6”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_15 -%}

    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}

{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 8.10.4 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX6”设置为“0:0:0:0:0:0:0:0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}

            {%- endif -%}

        {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
```

### 9 配置UPF（UEG）到园区2（LocalDN2）的IP路由数据。

#### 9.1 配置VPN实例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_l3vpninst_list_3 -%}
    ADD L3VPNINST:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_vpninstaf_list_6 -%}
    ADD VPNINSTAF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFRD is not none -%}
        VRFRD="{{item.VRFRD}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.2 配置Loopback接口IP。用于建立BGP邻居。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_interface_list_3 -%}
    ADD INTERFACE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_ipbindvpn_list_3 -%}
    ADD IPBINDVPN:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_ifipv4address_list_3 -%}
    ADD IFIPV4ADDRESS:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    IFIPADDR="{{item.IFIPADDR}}",
    SUBNETMASK="{{item.SUBNETMASK}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRTYPE is not none -%}
        ADDRTYPE={{item.ADDRTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_ifipv6enable_list_3 -%}
    SET IFIPV6ENABLE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    ENABLEFLAG={{item.ENABLEFLAG}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_ifipv6address_list_3 -%}
    ADD IFIPV6ADDRESS:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    IFNAME="{{item.IFNAME}}",
    IPV6ADDRESS="{{item.IPV6ADDRESS}}",
    PREFIXLEN={{item.PREFIXLEN}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.3 配置BGP VPN实例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgpvrf_list_3 -%}
    ADD BGPVRF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.DEFAULTAFTYPE is not none -%}
        DEFAULTAFTYPE={{item.DEFAULTAFTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ROUTERID is not none -%}
        ROUTERID="{{item.ROUTERID}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.4 增加BGP VPN实例地址族。UEG对接2个负荷分担的BGP PEER时，“MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE”设置为“2”。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgpvrfaf_list_6 -%}
    ADD BGPVRFAF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=ipv4uni/ipv6uni 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE in ['ipv4uni', 'ipv6uni'] -%}
        {%- if item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE is not none -%}
            MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE={{item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.5 配置BGP对等体。此处以对接2个IPv4v6双栈的BGP对等体为例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgppeer_list_12 -%}
    ADD BGPPEER:
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE is not none -%}
        ADDRESSTYPE={{item.ADDRESSTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4'] -%}
        PEERADDR="{{item.PEERADDR}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=ipv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE == 'ipv6' -%}
        PEERADDRV6="{{item.PEERADDRV6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.REMOTEAS is not none -%}
            REMOTEAS="{{item.REMOTEAS}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFADDR is not none -%}
            LOCALIFADDR="{{item.LOCALIFADDR}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFNAME is not none -%}
            LOCALIFNAME="{{item.LOCALIFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.EBGPMAXHOP is not none -%}
            EBGPMAXHOP={{item.EBGPMAXHOP}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.6 配置BGP引入wlr路由。

##### 9.6.1 //UPF采用主备容灾方案时，需要将“MEDENABLE”设置为“TRUE”，且备UPF的“MED”值必须大于主UPF的MED值。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_importroute_list_6 -%}

    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    IMPORTPROTOCOL={{item.IMPORTPROTOCOL}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.MEDENABLE is not none -%}
        MEDENABLE={{item.MEDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```


```jinja2

    ADD AUTOSCALINGSERVICE:

    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",

    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}

    {%- endif -%}

    {%- endif -%}

    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：AUTOCFGIFTYPE=ETHTRUNK 时必选 --- -#}

        ETHTRUNKTMPID={{item.ETHTRUNKTMPID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}

        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE4={{item.IPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv6' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE6={{item.IPALLOCTYPE6}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPALLOCTYPE6=DHCP 时可选 --- -#}

{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.9 增加BFD会话自动部署模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingbfd_list_6 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGBFD:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    TEMPLATENAME="{{item.TEMPLATENAME}}",
    BFDTYPE={{item.BFDTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.DETECTMULTI is not none -%}
        DETECTMULTI={{item.DETECTMULTI}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：BFDTYPE=Static 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：BFDTYPE=Static 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        DESTADDR4="{{item.DESTADDR4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        DESTADDR6="{{item.DESTADDR6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDTYPE=Static 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        {%- if item.MINECHORXINT is not none -%}
            MINECHORXINT={{item.MINECHORXINT}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDTYPE=Static 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDTYPE == 'Static' -%}
        {%- if item.ONEARMECHO is not none -%}
            ONEARMECHO={{item.ONEARMECHO}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 9.10 增加静态路由自动部署模板。

##### 9.10.1 //“PREFIX4”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_18 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
        BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
        DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
            BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 9.10.2 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX4”设置为“0.0.0.0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_20 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
        MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}

            VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.PREFERENCE is not none -%}
            PREFERENCE={{item.PREFERENCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
            BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
            DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            PREFIX4="{{item.PREFIX4}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv4' -%}
            {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
                NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE4}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
            {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
                BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

##### 9.10.3 //“PREFIX6”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。


#### 10.3 配置BGP VPN实例。

```jinja2

    {%- endif -%}

{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 10.4 增加BGP VPN实例地址族。UEG对接2个负荷分担的BGP PEER时，“MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE”设置为“2”。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgpvrfaf_list_8 -%}
    ADD BGPVRFAF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=ipv4uni/ipv6uni 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE in ['ipv4uni', 'ipv6uni'] -%}
        {%- if item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE is not none -%}
            MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE={{item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 10.5 配置BGP对等体。此处以对接2个IPv4v6双栈的BGP对等体为例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgppeer_list_16 -%}
    ADD BGPPEER:
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE is not none -%}
        ADDRESSTYPE={{item.ADDRESSTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4'] -%}
        PEERADDR="{{item.PEERADDR}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=ipv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE == 'ipv6' -%}
        PEERADDRV6="{{item.PEERADDRV6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.REMOTEAS is not none -%}
            REMOTEAS="{{item.REMOTEAS}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFADDR is not none -%}
            LOCALIFADDR="{{item.LOCALIFADDR}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFNAME is not none -%}
            LOCALIFNAME="{{item.LOCALIFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.EBGPMAXHOP is not none -%}
            EBGPMAXHOP={{item.EBGPMAXHOP}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 10.6 配置BGP引入wlr路由。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_importroute_list_8 -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingservice_list_8 -%}

    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}

    {%- endif -%}

    {#- --- 条件可选：IPALLOCTYPE6=DHCP 时可选 --- -#}

    {%- endif -%}
    ;
```

##### 10.10.2 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX4”设置为“0.0.0.0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_26 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}

        {%- endif -%}

        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}

        {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
            {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
                BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

##### 10.10.3 //“PREFIX6”和“MASKLENGTH”是目的IP和掩码（BGP对等体的IP地址和掩码）。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_27 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
        BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
        DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        PREFIX6="{{item.PREFIX6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
        {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
            NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
        {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
            BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 10.10.4 //对端网元不发布BGP默认路由时，需要配置该步骤。“PREFIX6”设置为“0:0:0:0:0:0:0:0”，“MASKLENGTH”设置为“0”。其他参数与到BGP对等体的路由相同。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_peer_no_bgp_default_route -%}
    {%- for item in add_autoscalingsrroute_list_29 -%}
        ADD AUTOSCALINGSRROUTE:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
        MASKLENGTH={{item.MASKLENGTH}},
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
            VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.PREFERENCE is not none -%}
            PREFERENCE={{item.PREFERENCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE is not none -%}
            BFDENABLE={{item.BFDENABLE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.DESCRIPTION is not none -%}
            DESCRIPTION="{{item.DESCRIPTION}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
            PREFIX6="{{item.PREFIX6}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPv6' -%}
            {%- if item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
                NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6={{item.NEXTHOPALLOCTYPE6}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：BFDENABLE=TRUE 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.BFDENABLE == 'TRUE' -%}
            {%- if item.BFDTEMPLATENAME is not none -%}
                BFDTEMPLATENAME="{{item.BFDTEMPLATENAME}}",
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

### 11 配置UPF（UEG）到MPF的IP路由数据（传输Mp2接口报文）。

#### 11.1 配置VPN实例。

```jinja2
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2

    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2

{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
    ;
```

#### 11.3 配置BGP VPN实例。

#### 11.4 增加BGP VPN实例地址族。UEG对接2个负荷分担的BGP PEER时，“MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE”设置为“2”。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgpvrfaf_list_10 -%}
    ADD BGPVRFAF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=ipv4uni/ipv6uni 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE in ['ipv4uni', 'ipv6uni'] -%}
        {%- if item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE is not none -%}
            MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE={{item.MAXIMUMLOADBALANCE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 11.5 配置BGP对等体。此处以对接2个IPv4v6双栈的BGP对等体为例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_bgppeer_list_20 -%}
    ADD BGPPEER:
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VRFNAME is not none -%}
        VRFNAME="{{item.VRFNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE is not none -%}
        ADDRESSTYPE={{item.ADDRESSTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4'] -%}
        PEERADDR="{{item.PEERADDR}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：ADDRESSTYPE=ipv6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE == 'ipv6' -%}
        PEERADDRV6="{{item.PEERADDRV6}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.REMOTEAS is not none -%}
            REMOTEAS="{{item.REMOTEAS}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFADDR is not none -%}
            LOCALIFADDR="{{item.LOCALIFADDR}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.LOCALIFNAME is not none -%}
            LOCALIFNAME="{{item.LOCALIFNAME}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：ADDRESSTYPE=noaf/ipv4/ipv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.ADDRESSTYPE in ['noaf', 'ipv4', 'ipv6'] -%}
        {%- if item.EBGPMAXHOP is not none -%}
            EBGPMAXHOP={{item.EBGPMAXHOP}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_importroute_list_10 -%}
```


#### 11.8 配置外联口自动部署模板。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_autoscalingservice_list_10 -%}
    ADD AUTOSCALINGSERVICE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SERVICENAME="{{item.SERVICENAME}}",
    AFTYPE={{item.AFTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.VPNNAME is not none -%}
        VPNNAME="{{item.VPNNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE is not none -%}
        AUTOCFGIFTYPE={{item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.VLANID is not none -%}
        VLANID={{item.VLANID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：AUTOCFGIFTYPE=ETHTRUNK 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AUTOCFGIFTYPE == 'ETHTRUNK' -%}
        ETHTRUNKTMPID={{item.ETHTRUNKTMPID}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv4 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv4' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE4 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE4={{item.IPALLOCTYPE4}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：AFTYPE=IPv6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.AFTYPE == 'IPv6' -%}
        {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 is not none -%}
            IPALLOCTYPE6={{item.IPALLOCTYPE6}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPALLOCTYPE6=DHCP 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPALLOCTYPE6 == 'DHCP' -%}
        {%- if item.ADDRMODE is not none -%}
            ADDRMODE={{item.ADDRMODE}},
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

### 12 打开自动部署开关。

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_autoconfig_list_1 -%}
    SET AUTOCONFIG:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    SWITCHFLAG={{item.SWITCHFLAG}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

### 从自动部署开关打开到自动部署功能生效有时延，因此执行其他操作前（例如手动部署路由互通、关闭自动部署开关），务必先执行**DSP OPSASSISTSTATE**命令查询自动部署维护助手“autoscaling\_autoconfig.py”的状态为“准备好状态（Ready）”，否则结果不可预期。

### 13 配置逻辑接口。

#### 13.1 配置Sa/N3/S1-U逻辑接口。

##### 13.1.1 配置Sa/N3/S1-U逻辑接口所属的VPN实例。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_vpninst_list -%}
    ADD VPNINST:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    VPNINSTANCE="{{item.VPNINSTANCE}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 13.1.2 配置Sa/N3/S1-U逻辑接口。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_logicinf_list -%}
    ADD LOGICINF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    NAME="{{item.NAME}}",
    IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV4/IPVER_ALL 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION in ['IPV4', 'IPVER_ALL'] -%}
        IPV4ADDRESS1="{{item.IPV4ADDRESS1}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV4/IPVER_ALL 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION in ['IPV4', 'IPVER_ALL'] -%}
        IPV4MASK1="{{item.IPV4MASK1}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPVER_ALL/IPV6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION in ['IPVER_ALL', 'IPV6'] -%}
        IPV6ADDRESS1="{{item.IPV6ADDRESS1}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPVER_ALL/IPV6 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION in ['IPVER_ALL', 'IPV6'] -%}
        IPV6PREFIXLEN1={{item.IPV6PREFIXLEN1}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件可选：IPVERSION=IPV4/IPVER_ALL/IPV6 时可选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.IPVERSION in ['IPV4', 'IPVER_ALL', 'IPV6'] -%}
        {%- if item.VPNINSTANCE is not none -%}
            VPNINSTANCE="{{item.VPNINSTANCE}}",
        {%- endif -%}
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 13.4.3 配置VXLAN隧道。如未配置APN，则ADD VXLANBINDAPN和ADD VPNVNIMAP会执行失败。

```jinja2
        HEARTBEAT={{item.HEARTBEAT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.TIMETHRESHOLD is not none -%}
        TIMETHRESHOLD={{item.TIMETHRESHOLD}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.HEARTSUCCLIMIT is not none -%}
        HEARTSUCCLIMIT={{item.HEARTSUCCLIMIT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.HEARTFAILLIMIT is not none -%}
        HEARTFAILLIMIT={{item.HEARTFAILLIMIT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.TIMEOUT is not none -%}
        TIMEOUT={{item.TIMEOUT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

##### 13.4.4 配置分流规则。

###### 13.4.4.1 //“SVRIP”/“SVRIPMASKLEN”是部署在MEP上的第三方APP服务器的IP地址和掩码长度。访问如下脚本指定的APP服务器（192.168.1.1）时，流量将通过VXLAN隧道发送到MPF；访问如下脚本没有指定的APP服务器（如192.168.2.2）时，则流量不经过MPF。UserProfilename需要与SMF上配置的UserProfilename一致。

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_filter_list -%}
    ADD FILTER:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FILTERNAME="{{item.FILTERNAME}}",
    L34PROTTYPE={{item.L34PROTTYPE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.SVRIPMODE is not none -%}
        SVRIPMODE={{item.SVRIPMODE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.MSSTARTPORT is not none -%}
        MSSTARTPORT={{item.MSSTARTPORT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.MSENDPORT is not none -%}
        MSENDPORT={{item.MSENDPORT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.SVRSTARTPORT is not none -%}
        SVRSTARTPORT={{item.SVRSTARTPORT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.SVRENDPORT is not none -%}
        SVRENDPORT={{item.SVRENDPORT}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：L34PROTTYPE=STRING 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.L34PROTTYPE == 'STRING' -%}
        L34PROTOCOL={{item.L34PROTOCOL}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：SVRIPMODE=IP 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.SVRIPMODE == 'IP' -%}
        SVRIP="{{item.SVRIP}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：SVRIPMODE=IP 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.SVRIPMODE == 'IP' -%}
        SVRIPMASKTYPE={{item.SVRIPMASKTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：SVRIPMASKTYPE=LENGTHTYPE 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.SVRIPMASKTYPE == 'LENGTHTYPE' -%}
        SVRIPMASKLEN={{item.SVRIPMASKLEN}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_flowfilter_list -%}
    ADD FLOWFILTER:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FLOWFILTERNAME="{{item.FLOWFILTERNAME}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.TETHERDETFLAG is not none -%}
        TETHERDETFLAG={{item.TETHERDETFLAG}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.FLOWFILTERTYPE is not none -%}
        FLOWFILTERTYPE={{item.FLOWFILTERTYPE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_fltbindflowf_list -%}
    ADD FLTBINDFLOWF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FLOWFILTERNAME="{{item.FLOWFILTERNAME}}",
    FILTERNAME="{{item.FILTERNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_rule_list -%}
    ADD RULE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    RULENAME="{{item.RULENAME}}",
    POLICYTYPE={{item.POLICYTYPE}},
    FILTERINGMODE={{item.FILTERINGMODE}},
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.PRIORITY is not none -%}
        PRIORITY={{item.PRIORITY}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：FILTERINGMODE=FLOWFILTER 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.FILTERINGMODE == 'FLOWFILTER' -%}
        FLOWFILTERNAME="{{item.FLOWFILTERNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：POLICYTYPE=WORKER 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.POLICYTYPE == 'WORKER' -%}
        WORKERNAME="{{item.WORKERNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_userprofile_list -%}
    ADD USERPROFILE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    USERPROFILENAME="{{item.USERPROFILENAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_rulebinding_list -%}
    ADD RULEBINDING:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    USERPROFILENAME="{{item.USERPROFILENAME}}",
    RULENAME="{{item.RULENAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
        FRAMEDROUTE={{item.FRAMEDROUTE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ANTISPOOFINGDL is not none -%}
        ANTISPOOFINGDL={{item.ANTISPOOFINGDL}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ANTISPOOFINGUL is not none -%}
        ANTISPOOFINGUL={{item.ANTISPOOFINGUL}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.IPTYPEFORDUALIP is not none -%}
        IPTYPEFORDUALIP={{item.IPTYPEFORDUALIP}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.SINGLEADDRCAUSE is not none -%}
        SINGLEADDRCAUSE={{item.SINGLEADDRCAUSE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.IPV6RAMTUSW is not none -%}

    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.IPV6RALIFETIME is not none -%}

    {%- endif -%}

{%- endfor -%}
```

#### 14.4 配置IP地址分配（UE地址使用动态分配地址）。此处以基于APN/DNN给UE分配地址为例。基于APN/DNN分配地址时，每个园区需要规划一个独立的网段。其他网元分配地址时，请跳过本步骤。

##### 14.4.1 //配置全局地址分配规则。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_dynamic_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in set_ipallocrule_list -%}
        SET IPALLOCRULE:
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.FIRSTRULESW is not none -%}
            FIRSTRULESW={{item.FIRSTRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.SECONDRULESW is not none -%}
            SECONDRULESW={{item.SECONDRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.THIRDRULESW is not none -%}
            THIRDRULESW={{item.THIRDRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6FIRSTRULESW is not none -%}
            IPV6FIRSTRULESW={{item.IPV6FIRSTRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6SECRULESW is not none -%}
            IPV6SECRULESW={{item.IPV6SECRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6THIRDRULESW is not none -%}
            IPV6THIRDRULESW={{item.IPV6THIRDRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：FIRSTRULESW=ENABLE 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.FIRSTRULESW == 'ENABLE' -%}
            FIRSTRULE={{item.FIRSTRULE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPV6FIRSTRULESW=ENABLE 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPV6FIRSTRULESW == 'ENABLE' -%}
            IPV6FIRSTRULE={{item.IPV6FIRSTRULE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

##### 14.4.2 //配置APN“apn-op1”给UE分配的IP地址段。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_dynamic_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in add_pool_list -%}
        ADD POOL:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        POOLNAME="{{item.POOLNAME}}",
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE is not none -%}
            POOLTYPE={{item.POOLTYPE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：HASVPN=ENABLE 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.HASVPN == 'ENABLE' -%}
            VPNINSTANCE="{{item.VPNINSTANCE}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=LOCAL/EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE in ['LOCAL', 'EXTERNAL'] -%}
            {%- if item.IPVERSION is not none -%}
                IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=LOCAL/EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE in ['LOCAL', 'EXTERNAL'] -%}
            {%- if item.HASVPN is not none -%}
                HASVPN={{item.HASVPN}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_dynamic_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in add_section_list -%}
        ADD SECTION:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        POOLNAME="{{item.POOLNAME}}",
        SECTIONNUM={{item.SECTIONNUM}},
        IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV4 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPV4' -%}
            V4STARTIP="{{item.V4STARTIP}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV4 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPV4' -%}
            V4ENDIP="{{item.V4ENDIP}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV6 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPV6' -%}
            V6PREFIXSTART="{{item.V6PREFIXSTART}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV6 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPV6' -%}
            V6PREFIXEND="{{item.V6PREFIXEND}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPVERSION=IPV6 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPVERSION == 'IPV6' -%}
            V6PREFIXLENGTH={{item.V6PREFIXLENGTH}},
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_dynamic_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in add_poolgroup_list -%}
        ADD POOLGROUP:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        POOLGRPNAME="{{item.POOLGRPNAME}}",
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPV4ALLOCPRIALG is not none -%}
            IPV4ALLOCPRIALG={{item.IPV4ALLOCPRIALG}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6ALLOCPRIALG is not none -%}
            IPV6ALLOCPRIALG={{item.IPV6ALLOCPRIALG}},
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_dynamic_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in add_poolbindgroup_list -%}
        ADD POOLBINDGROUP:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        POOLGROUPNAME="{{item.POOLGROUPNAME}}",
        POOLNAME="{{item.POOLNAME}}",
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.PRIORITY is not none -%}

        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_dynamic_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in add_poolgrpmap_list -%}
        ADD POOLGRPMAP:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        MAPPINGNAME="{{item.MAPPINGNAME}}",
        POOLGROUPNAME="{{item.POOLGROUPNAME}}",
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
```

#### 14.5 配置IP地址分配（UE地址使用静态签约地址）。使用UPF动态给UE分配IP时，请跳过本步骤。

#### 静态签约场景，配置IP地址分配用于对外发布wlr\_ud路由，避免静态用户激活后生成大量主机路由。地址段从UDM签约数据获取。

##### 14.5.1 //配置全局地址分配规则。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_static_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in set_ipallocrule_list_1 -%}
        SET IPALLOCRULE:
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.FIRSTRULESW is not none -%}
            FIRSTRULESW={{item.FIRSTRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.SECONDRULESW is not none -%}
            SECONDRULESW={{item.SECONDRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.THIRDRULESW is not none -%}
            THIRDRULESW={{item.THIRDRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6FIRSTRULESW is not none -%}
            IPV6FIRSTRULESW={{item.IPV6FIRSTRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6SECRULESW is not none -%}
            IPV6SECRULESW={{item.IPV6SECRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {%- if item.IPV6THIRDRULESW is not none -%}
            IPV6THIRDRULESW={{item.IPV6THIRDRULESW}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：FIRSTRULESW=ENABLE 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.FIRSTRULESW == 'ENABLE' -%}
            FIRSTRULE={{item.FIRSTRULE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选：IPV6FIRSTRULESW=ENABLE 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.IPV6FIRSTRULESW == 'ENABLE' -%}
            IPV6FIRSTRULE={{item.IPV6FIRSTRULE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

##### 14.5.2 //配置APN“apn-op1”给UE分配的IP地址段。

```jinja2
{%- if is_scenario_static_ip_allocation -%}
    {%- for item in add_pool_list_4 -%}
        ADD POOL:
        {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
        POOLNAME="{{item.POOLNAME}}",
        {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE is not none -%}
            POOLTYPE={{item.POOLTYPE}},
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件必选：HASVPN=ENABLE 时必选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.HASVPN == 'ENABLE' -%}
            VPNINSTANCE="{{item.VPNINSTANCE}}",
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选参数 --- -#}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=LOCAL/EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE in ['LOCAL', 'EXTERNAL'] -%}
            {%- if item.IPVERSION is not none -%}
                IPVERSION={{item.IPVERSION}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE == 'EXTERNAL' -%}
            {%- if item.CHECKIPVALID is not none -%}
                CHECKIPVALID={{item.CHECKIPVALID}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=LOCAL/EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE in ['LOCAL', 'EXTERNAL'] -%}
            {%- if item.HASVPN is not none -%}
                HASVPN={{item.HASVPN}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=LOCAL/EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE in ['LOCAL', 'EXTERNAL'] -%}
            {%- if item.IMSSW is not none -%}
                IMSSW={{item.IMSSW}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        {#- --- 条件可选：POOLTYPE=EXTERNAL 时可选 --- -#}
        {%- if item.POOLTYPE == 'EXTERNAL' -%}
            {%- if item.REDUNDFUNC is not none -%}
                REDUNDFUNC={{item.REDUNDFUNC}},
            {%- endif -%}
        {%- endif -%}
        ;
    {%- endfor -%}
{%- endif -%}
```

#### 14.6 配置本地PCC规则。

##### 14.6.1 //如下脚本必须配置，否则会话建立失败。脚本中参数值“up\_test”/“test”/“test\_ipv6”可修改为实际规划的值，替换时请注意替换完整，其他参数使用样例值。UserProfilename需要与SMF上配置的UserProfilename一致。

#### 当需要计费时，配置**ADD PCCPOLICYGRP**命令时需要设置参数“URR组名称（URRGROUPNAME）”。

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_licenseswitch_list -%}
    SET LICENSESWITCH:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    LICITEM="{{item.LICITEM}}",
    SWITCH={{item.SWITCH}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_filter_list_1 -%}
    ADD FILTER:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FILTERNAME="{{item.FILTERNAME}}",
    L34PROTTYPE={{item.L34PROTTYPE}},
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：L34PROTTYPE=STRING 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.L34PROTTYPE == 'STRING' -%}
        L34PROTOCOL={{item.L34PROTOCOL}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_filteripv6_list -%}
    ADD FILTERIPV6:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FILTERNAME="{{item.FILTERNAME}}",
    L34PROTTYPEV6={{item.L34PROTTYPEV6}},
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：L34PROTTYPEV6=STRING 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.L34PROTTYPEV6 == 'STRING' -%}
        L34PROTOCOL={{item.L34PROTOCOL}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_flowfilter_list_1 -%}
    ADD FLOWFILTER:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FLOWFILTERNAME="{{item.FLOWFILTERNAME}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.TETHERDETFLAG is not none -%}
        TETHERDETFLAG={{item.TETHERDETFLAG}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_fltbindflowf_list_1 -%}
    ADD FLTBINDFLOWF:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    FLOWFILTERNAME="{{item.FLOWFILTERNAME}}",
    FILTERNAME="{{item.FILTERNAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in set_refreshsrv_list -%}
    SET REFRESHSRV:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    REFRESHTYPE={{item.REFRESHTYPE}},
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_pccpolicygrp_list -%}
    ADD PCCPOLICYGRP:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    PCCPOLICYGRPNM="{{item.PCCPOLICYGRPNM}}",
    {#- --- 可选参数 --- -#}
    {%- if item.FUPSESSIONEXC is not none -%}
        FUPSESSIONEXC={{item.FUPSESSIONEXC}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.TOKENFUNCFLAG is not none -%}
        TOKENFUNCFLAG={{item.TOKENFUNCFLAG}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.SIGNALASSOCIATE is not none -%}
        SIGNALASSOCIATE={{item.SIGNALASSOCIATE}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.ADCMUTEFLAG is not none -%}
        ADCMUTEFLAG={{item.ADCMUTEFLAG}},
    {%- endif -%}
    {%- if item.HTTP2DEGRADESW is not none -%}
        HTTP2DEGRADESW={{item.HTTP2DEGRADESW}},
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_rule_list_1 -%}
    ADD RULE:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    RULENAME="{{item.RULENAME}}",
    POLICYTYPE={{item.POLICYTYPE}},
    FILTERINGMODE={{item.FILTERINGMODE}},
    {#- --- 条件必选参数 --- -#}
    {#- --- 条件必选：FILTERINGMODE=FLOWFILTER 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.FILTERINGMODE == 'FLOWFILTER' -%}
        FLOWFILTERNAME="{{item.FLOWFILTERNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    {#- --- 条件必选：POLICYTYPE=PCC/BWM/HEADEN/SMARTREDIRECT/TRAFFICCLASS/FIREWALL/QOS/WEBPROXY 时必选 --- -#}
    {%- if item.POLICYTYPE in ['PCC', 'BWM', 'HEADEN', 'SMARTREDIRECT', 'TRAFFICCLASS', 'FIREWALL', 'QOS', 'WEBPROXY'] -%}
        POLICYNAME="{{item.POLICYNAME}}",
    {%- endif -%}
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```

```jinja2
{%- for item in add_rulebinding_list_1 -%}
    ADD RULEBINDING:
    {#- --- 必选参数 --- -#}
    USERPROFILENAME="{{item.USERPROFILENAME}}",
    RULENAME="{{item.RULENAME}}",
    ;
{%- endfor -%}
```
